fix-restart merged: a d2d game-lost recovery now counts in the minute-line restarts (lost restarts and recycles are disjoint, no double count; Game.exe unchanged); d2d SavedExp is read from the managed d2d home save file (re-read when mtime/size change): xpRisk = XP since the last save instead of the whole XP (an attached d2d has no managed home: SavedExp stays nil); not verified live: that the save file sits under the d2d home the way the drill harness finds it; live proof = rerun D10 (`-only D10`).
fix-nav merged: post-fix soak finding classified: waypoint-to-town 33 attempts / 18 failures: 16 `level changed during the walk 6 -> 20` (Black Marsh into the Forgotten Tower): NOT a path crossing an exit (the plan avoids exits): the character arrives in the tower mouth trigger and the step-away repeated the identical step 5+ times: fixed: a step-away that ends in another level counts as a bounce and the next one turns direction; 78 leveling target not reachable lines: Black Marsh was touched by the town waypoint trip at 15:23 but the runner never learned of it (missing hand-off: fixed: DrainActivated), Dark Wood had 0 touches in 11 visits: UNVERIFIED why, picker and the 70-subtile limit unchanged; level-relative waypoint positions for towns 75, 103, 109 added (d2d only); economy driver (143 empty-source portal attempts) untouched: policy; live proof = DW.m1 DW.m2 DW.a12 DW.a13

fix-belt2 merged (shared): post-fix soak finding: 47 of 190 belt fills moved 0 (42 mana, 7 healing, 7 rejuvenation, all on an 8-box sash): cause was NOT the plan (a Sorceress is hp,hp,mp,mp) nor Place/pickBeltBox: ground pickups took any potion the game would belt with no count limit, healing filled the planned mana column, then the belt read full for mana and the sticky full mark made it worse (packMP above 0 in 570 of 1,253 minutes; belt had no mana in 598 minutes, no healing in 28): fixed: no belt column = no room (no sticky mark), PlaceFor/WillBeltFor keep a planned kind a column, ground healing that would take the last mana column is left; OPEN POLICY QUESTION for the user: how many mana columns should a Sorceress have (today 2 of 4 at every belt size below clvl 31; SME advice UNVERIFIED: 1+1 on 4 and 8 boxes, 2+2 on 12 and 16); whether d2d drinks pack mana is UNVERIFIED; live proof = the three drill variants: P1 on a Sorceress sash with 6 healing and 4 mana in the pack and an empty belt; P7 with healing on the ground while healing fills 3 columns; a fill with a mana potion and no free box expects no 'marked full' line.

primitives review merged (docs/primitives-review.md): hypothesis PARTIAL: the belt move works (335 of 361 fills succeed) but every failed fill (67 of 67) is hand: item not found right after a save-and-exit re-entry and sets the sticky beltFullAt which is never reset on re-entry (F1); recovery-mode pickup is never on because the looter adapter drops SetRecovering (F2); no belt step after the shop (F3); d2dPortal clicks an empty tome and HasTP counts an empty tome (F4, F5); the kit gate ignores healing potions in the pack (F6); combat BeltOf drops belt boxes x>=4 (F7); freeBeltBox ignores column kind (F8); open F9: why the item id vanishes right after re-entry; fix cards pending the user's go
The next milestone is making the bot itself better: the town and inventory basics must be rock solid on d2d, not progression through the game. The d2-sme's 15-scenario checklist (2026-10-31) is the acceptance list; each scenario needs an offline test or a live proof, tracked in a coverage table (first step: the offline basics audit, awaiting the user's go).
1. Gold 50, belt empty, in town: buys 1 healing potion, no scroll or tome, leaves.
2. Gold 120, 1 scroll, belt full: buys no scroll, keeps the gold, no shop visit.
3. Gold 300, no scroll, belt full, clvl 9: buys 1 scroll, keeps at least 150.
4. One junk item worth 20 gold, 0 scrolls: no sell trip.
5. Seven sellable items worth over 30 gold in total: one visit sells all, scroll count unchanged.
6. Charm, rune and gem in the pack: all still held after a sell visit.
7. Unidentified magic item worth under 100: sold unidentified, no ID scroll bought.
8. Durability 28%, gold 200: repaired in the same visit, no extra trip.
9. Durability 90%: no repair attempt.
10. Repair fails: logged once, no retry until gold or durability changes.
11. Gold 1500 at clvl 5: the stash step banks the surplus, the pack keeps 300 + 50 x clvl.
12. Belt has 2 free slots and 1 spare potion in the pack: the spare goes to the belt before leaving town.
13. Vendor armor needs more Strength than the character has: not bought, not bought again after a restart.
14. Pack full (4 or fewer free cells): one sell visit, fresh loot ends in the pack.
15. Blood Raven done, clvl 8 or later: Kashya's reward claimed once, no second hire attempt.
Why this first: the SME read the poverty loop (about 54 % of time farming gold) as a spending-policy problem, so closing these basics is also expected to close most of the fidelity-gate gap (card `xpgap`, archived, section 1). Open unknowns: the starting gold of a new offline 1.14d character, real unidentified-item sell prices.

## 3. Needs the user (also the board's Waiting-on-you panel, `tools/needs-you.sh list`)
- The five decisions in [plan/STATUS.md](plan/STATUS.md): licence/GPL review, public character names, grill 0003 (merc and Act 2), upstream PR follow-ups (post the Lut Gholein PR; cast follow-ups wait for the maintainer), stream and ports.
- Kick stream stays OFF until asked. The container installer viewer needs a second port. The NAS autostart for d2-board.

## Parked by grill 0003 (ADR 0011, 2026-10-31)
- Build merc targets (each build's target merc and gear): needed before the keeper rule has keepers (card m3-merc).
- Desert merc in Normal (M4): buy or not, at what gold, survival in Act 2.
- Max-life floor for Andariel: waits for m3-andariel's life-per-phase numbers (cards m3-routeguard, m3-readiness-model).
- Live probes: dead-merc forfeit of the free Rogue, one revive quote, 2 to 3 hire windows, merc Str and Dex own-stats rule, merc state after quit and reload (card m3-merc).
- Readiness model weights and safety margin (card m3-readiness-model, deferred until m3-andariel has won once).
